{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01NKHGC1jMuu5NRosVfTq7NT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01T8JaPV5pb7rmbnD81YDA8Q","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /root/IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.txt << 'EOF'\n================================================================================\nINVESTMENT SPENDING SHOCK ANALYSIS FOR GEORGIA\nExcel Model Implementation Summary\n================================================================================\n\nPROJECT OVERVIEW\n================================================================================\nThis project estimates the impact of a $6.5 billion annual investment spending\nshock (2026-2033, total $52 billion) on Georgia's potential GDP using a \nCobb-Douglas production function framework.\n\nMETHODOLOGY\n================================================================================\n1. PRODUCTION FUNCTION APPROACH\n   - Cobb-Douglas: Y = A * K^α * L^(1-α)\n   - Capital share (α) = 0.35\n   - Total Factor Productivity (A) extracted via HP filtering\n   - Capital stock extended using perpetual inventory method\n\n2. HP FILTERING\n   - Smoothing parameter (λ) = 1600 (standard for annual data)\n   - Extracts trend TFP from output growth\n   - Solves optimization problem: minimize Σ(y-trend)² + λΣ(Δ²trend)²\n\n3. INVESTMENT SHOCK SCENARIO\n   - Annual investment: $6.5 billion USD\n   - Period: 2026-2033 (8 years)\n   - Total investment: $52 billion\n   - Capital accumulation: K_t = K_{t-1}*(1-δ) + I_t\n   - δ (depreciation rate) from ECB consumption of fixed capital\n\nDATA SOURCES\n================================================================================\n\n1. WEO_Data Sheet\n   Source: IMF World Economic Outlook Database\n   URL: https://data.imf.org/en/datasets/IMF.RES:WEO\n   Data:\n   - Real GDP growth rates 2000-2025\n   - Projections: 2026-2027 from WEO\n   - Extended: 2028+ at constant 6.2% growth rate\n   Time Period: 2000-2027 (actual), 2028-2041 (extended)\n\n2. CFC data Sheet\n   Source: ECB Data Portal - IDCM Dataset\n   URL: https://data.ecb.europa.eu/data/geographical-areas/georgia\n   Data:\n   - Consumption of Fixed Capital (P51C indicator)\n   - Currency: Georgian Lari (millions)\n   - Time Period: 1996-2023\n   - Updated: May 29, 2024\n   Usage: Calculate depreciation rate (δ)\n\n3. PWT Sheet\n   Source: Penn World Table 11.0 (Groningen Growth & Development Centre)\n   URL: https://www.rug.nl/ggdc/productivity/pwt/\n   Accessed via: FRED (Federal Reserve Economic Data)\n   Data:\n   - Capital stock at current PPPs (CKSPPPGEA666NRUG)\n   - Real GDP at constant national prices (RGDPNAGEA666NRUG)\n   - TFP level at current PPPs\n   Time Period: 1990-2019\n\n4. Investment Sheet\n   Source: User-specified shock scenario\n   Parameters:\n   - Annual amount: $6.5 billion\n   - Start: 2026\n   - Duration: 8 years\n   - Total: $52 billion\n\nWORKBOOK STRUCTURE\n================================================================================\n\nSHEET 1: PWT (Penn World Table)\n- Row 2: Variable definitions\n- Rows 3-7: Key Georgia economic indicators from PWT\n- Data: Capital stock, real GDP, TFP, employment\n\nSHEET 2: WEO_Data (IMF World Economic Outlook)\n- Row 3: Headers\n- Rows 4-31: Years 2000-2027\n- Column A: Year\n- Column B: Real GDP (national currency, billions)\n- Column C: Real GDP growth rate (%)\n- Column D: Projected/extended real GDP\n- Formulas: Y_t = Y_{t-1} * (1 + g_t/100)\n\nSHEET 3: CFC data (ECB Consumption of Fixed Capital)\n- Row 3: Headers\n- Rows 4-31: Years 1996-2023\n- Column A: Year\n- Column B: CFC (millions LR)\n- Column C: CFC as share of GDP\n- Column D: Depreciation rate calculation\n- Formula (B3): =AVERAGE(CFC_data!D20:D28) → average δ over 2015-2023\n\nSHEET 4: Production (HP Filter & Growth Accounting)\n- Row 3: Average Depreciation Rate (B3 = output of CFC analysis)\n- Rows 8-29: Years 2002-2023 (22 years for HP filter)\n- Column C: Capital stock (K)\n- Column D: Real GDP (Y)\n- Column E: LnK (log capital)\n- Column F: LnY (log output)\n- Column G: LnZ (initial TFP, before HP filtering)\n- Column H: LnZ_Trend (HP trend, updated post-optimization)\n- Column L: LnZ_HP (HP filtered values, Solver variable)\n- Column M: Residuals (data - trend)\n- Column N: HP objective components (residuals² + λ*2nd diff²)\n- Cell P6: Solver objective = Σ(residuals²) + λΣ(Δ²trend)²\n- Cell Q3: Lambda parameter = 1600\n\nSOLVER SETUP:\n- Target: Minimize P6\n- By changing: L8:L29 (LnZ_HP values)\n- After optimization: Copy L to H (LnZ_Trend)\n\nSHEET 5: Investment\n- Rows 4-6: Investment parameters\n- Rows 11-18: Investment schedule 2026-2033\n- Column A: Year\n- Column B: Annual investment = $6.5 billion\n- Column C: Cumulative investment\n\nSHEET 6: Production_Function\n- Rows 4-43: Years 2002-2041 (40 years)\n- Columns A-B: Year index\n- Column C: K (capital stock, 2002-2023 from Production sheet)\n- Column D: K/Y ratios (for anchor estimation)\n- Column E: K extended (historical + extended using avg K/Y)\n- Column F: Y extended (GDP from WEO + extended forecast)\n- Column G: LnZ_Trend (HP filtered TFP trend from Production sheet)\n- Column H: Capital share (α) = 0.35\n- Column I: Annual investment (from Investment sheet)\n- Column J: K_with (capital with investment shock)\n  Formula: K_with_t = K_with_{t-1}*(1-δ) + I_t\n- Column K: Y response to capital (for production function)\n- Column L: Ystar_base = exp(A_trend) * K_extended^α\n- Column M: Ystar_with = exp(A_trend) * K_with^α\n- Column N: Output gain = Ystar_with - Ystar_base\n\nPARAMETERS:\n- P3: Capital share (α) = 0.35\n- P5: Labor share (1-α) = 0.65\n- P7: Average K/Y ratio (2015-2023)\n\nSHEET 7: Summary\n- Key parameters and results\n- Output effects 2023 vs 2041\n- Cumulative output gains\n- Marginal output per dollar invested\n\nSHEET 8: Documentation\n- Detailed explanation of all sheets\n- Calculation methodology\n- Parameter definitions\n- Model assumptions\n\nSHEET 9: Execution Guide\n- Step-by-step instructions for running the model\n- Solver configuration\n- Result interpretation\n\nKEY FORMULAS\n================================================================================\n\n1. DEPRECIATION RATE (Production!B3)\n   =AVERAGE(CFC_data!D20:D28)\n   Calculates average δ from most recent 8 years of CFC/K ratios\n\n2. REAL GDP PROJECTION (WEO_Data!D column)\n   Row 4: =B4\n   Row 5+: =D{t-1}*(1+C{t}/100)\n   Chains growth rates forward\n\n3. CAPITAL STOCK EXTENSION (Production_Function!E column)\n   Rows 2002-2023: =B{row} (actual)\n   Rows 2024+: =$P$7*F{row} (K/Y anchor * Y)\n\n4. OUTPUT PROJECTION (Production_Function!F column)\n   2000-2027: =WEO_Data!D{row}\n   2028+: =F{row-1}*1.062 (constant 6.2% growth)\n\n5. HP FILTER OBJECTIVE (Production!P6)\n   =SUMPRODUCT(M8:M29,M8:M29)+$Q$3*SUMPRODUCT(I8:I29,I8:I29)\n   First term: Σ(residuals²)\n   Second term: λ*Σ(second differences²)\n\n6. COBB-DOUGLAS OUTPUT (Production_Function!L/M columns)\n   Ystar_base (L): =EXP(G)*E^$P$3\n   Ystar_with (M): =EXP(G)*J^$P$3\n   Capital accumulation (J): =J{t-1}*(1-δ)+I{t}\n\nCALCULATION WORKFLOW\n================================================================================\n\nSTEP 1: DATA VERIFICATION (5 minutes)\n- Open test-supply.xlsx\n- Check WEO_Data sheet for 2000-2027 coverage\n- Check CFC data sheet for 1996-2023 coverage\n- Verify Investment sheet: $6.5B annually, 2026-2033\n\nSTEP 2: HP FILTER OPTIMIZATION (10-15 minutes)\n- Go to Production sheet\n- Check P6 value (HP Filter objective)\n- Open Solver: Data > Solver\n  Settings:\n  - Target cell: $P$6\n  - Equal To: Min\n  - By changing cells: $L$8:$L$29\n  - Engine: GRG Nonlinear (default)\n- Click Solve\n- Excel will iterate to minimize objective\n- Acceptance: Default (click OK)\n\nSTEP 3: COPY SOLVER RESULTS (2 minutes)\n- Production sheet, Column L (L8:L29) now has optimized HP values\n- Copy L8:L29\n- Select H8:H29\n- Paste Special > Values\n- This propagates LnZ_Trend to Production_Function calculations\n\nSTEP 4: REVIEW RESULTS (5 minutes)\n- Go to Summary sheet: Overall impact metrics\n- Go to Production_Function sheet: Year-by-year impacts\n- Column N: Annual output gains from investment\n- Last row (2041): Long-term impact of investment shock\n\nINTERPRETATION\n================================================================================\n\nOUTPUT GAIN COMPONENTS:\n\n1. Direct Capital Effect\n   Investment → Higher capital stock → Higher output via Cobb-Douglas\n\n2. Magnitude\n   Given α=0.35, doubling capital increases output by 35%\n   Investment shock: ~20% capital increase → ~7% output effect\n\n3. Timeline\n   2026-2033: Investment period (output gains rising)\n   2033-2041: Post-investment (output gains persist, gradually depreciate)\n\n4. Efficiency Metric\n   Marginal product of capital (MPK) = α * Y/K\n   Output gain per dollar invested typically 0.05-0.15 (varies with baseline K/Y)\n\nASSUMPTIONS & LIMITATIONS\n================================================================================\n\n1. HP FILTERING\n   - Assumes trend TFP follows smooth path\n   - λ=1600 standard for annual data (sensitive to choice)\n   - Not robust to structural breaks\n\n2. CAPITAL ACCUMULATION\n   - Linear depreciation (δ constant)\n   - No adjustment costs or lags\n   - Investment immediately productive\n\n3. PRODUCTION FUNCTION\n   - Cobb-Douglas assumes constant returns to scale\n   - Capital share α=0.35 fixed (typical for developing economies)\n   - No labor substitution effects\n\n4. EXTENDED FORECASTS\n   - WEO projections (2026-2027) reliable\n   - Extended growth (2028-2041) assumes 6.2% constant\n   - Doesn't account for convergence dynamics or policy changes\n\n5. GEORGIA-SPECIFIC\n   - Data quality varies by series\n   - PWT only through 2019 (estimated 2020-2023)\n   - Currency conversion approximations\n\nFILES & REFERENCES\n================================================================================\n\nMain File: test-supply.xlsx (21 KB)\nLocation: /root/test-supply.xlsx\n\nData Sources URLs:\n- IMF WEO: https://data.imf.org/en/datasets/IMF.RES:WEO\n- ECB IDCM: https://data.ecb.europa.eu/data/geographical-areas/georgia\n- PWT 11.0: https://www.rug.nl.ggdc/productivity/pwt/\n- FRED PWT: https://fred.stlouisfed.org/release?rid=285\n\nSkills Documentation:\n- excel-economic-modeling.md (HP Filter, Cobb-Douglas implementation)\n- web-data-collection.md (Data access from IMF, ECB, PWT)\n- economic-concepts-modeling.md (Theoretical framework)\n\nCUSTOMIZATION OPTIONS\n================================================================================\n\nTo modify for different scenarios:\n\n1. CHANGE INVESTMENT AMOUNT\n   Investment!B4: Change from 6500 to desired amount (billions USD)\n   \n2. CHANGE INVESTMENT PERIOD\n   Investment!B5: Change start year\n   Investment!B6: Change duration\n   Update Production_Function formulas for year range\n\n3. ADJUST CAPITAL SHARE\n   Production_Function!P3: Change from 0.35\n   All Ystar formulas auto-update\n\n4. EXTEND PROJECTION PERIOD\n   Add rows to WEO_Data, Production, Production_Function\n   Copy formulas down to new rows\n\n5. INCORPORATE LABOR RESPONSE\n   Add employment series to Production sheet\n   Modify Ystar = exp(A)*K^α*L^(1-α)\n   Re-run HP Filter\n\n================================================================================\nCOMPLETION: January 29, 2026\nStatus: READY FOR USE\n================================================================================\nEOF\ncat /root/IMPLEMENTATION_SUMMARY.txt\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":163,"cache_read_input_tokens":51924,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":163,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":7},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3e9eb62-96a7-4e81-a399-d9247689a4b2","uuid":"c309d027-e130-469f-b565-c26660f23920"}

Efficiency Metric\n   Marginal product of capital (MPK) = α * Y/K\n   Output gain per dollar invested typically 0.05-0.15 (varies with baseline K/Y)\n\nASSUMPTIONS & LIMITATIONS\n================================================================================\n\n1. CAPITAL ACCUMULATION\n   - Linear depreciation (δ constant)\n   - No adjustment costs or lags\n   - Investment immediately productive\n\n3. PRODUCTION FUNCTION APPROACH\n   - Cobb-Douglas: Y = A * K^α * L^(1-α)\n   - Capital share (α) = 0.35\n   - Total Factor Productivity (A) extracted via HP filtering\n   - Capital stock extended using perpetual inventory method\n\n2. HP FILTERING\n   - Smoothing parameter (λ) = 1600 (standard for annual data)\n   - Extracts trend TFP from output growth\n   - Solves optimization problem: minimize Σ(y-trend)² + λΣ(Δ²trend)²\n\n3. INVESTMENT SHOCK SCENARIO\n   - Annual investment: $6.5 billion USD\n   - Period: 2026-2033 (8 years)\n   - Total investment: $52 billion\n   - Capital accumulation: K_t = K_{t-1}*(1-δ) + I_t\n   - δ (depreciation rate) from ECB consumption of fixed capital\n\nDATA SOURCES\n================================================================================\n\n1. Transfer Results (2 minutes)\nAfter Solver completes:\n1. Select cells **L8:L29** (optimized HP values)\n2. Copy\n3. Select **H8:H29**\n4. Paste Special → Values\n5. This updates the trend TFP (LnZ_Trend)\n\n### 5. Review Results\n- **Summary** sheet: Overall impact metrics\n- **Production_Function** sheet: Year-by-year output gains\n\n## Model Structure\n\n### Key Sheets\n\n| Sheet | Purpose | Years | Key Data |\n|-------|---------|-------|----------|\n| WEO_Data | Real GDP projections | 2000-2041 | GDP growth rates |\n| CFC data | Capital depreciation | 1996-2023 | Consumption of fixed capital |\n| Production | HP Filter optimization | 2002-2023 | TFP extraction |\n| Investment | Investment shock | 2026-2033 | $6.5B/year |\n| Production_Function | Cobb-Douglas analysis | 2002-2041 | Output impacts |\n| Summary | Results summary | Key metrics | Impact analysis |\n\n### Core Formulas\n\n**Depreciation Rate** (Production!B3):\n```\n=AVERAGE(CFC_data!D20:D28)\n```\nCalculates average δ from most recent 8 years\n\n**HP Filter Objective** (Production!P6):\n```\n=SUMPRODUCT(M8:M29,M8:M29)+$Q$3*SUMPRODUCT(I8:I29,I8:I29)\n```\nMinimizes: Σ(residuals²) + λ·Σ(second differences²)\n\n**Cobb-Douglas Output** (Production_Function):\n```\nYstar_base (L): =EXP(G)*E^$P$3\nYstar_with (M): =EXP(G)*J^$P$3\n```\nWhere:\n- G = HP filtered TFP trend (LnA)\n- E = Extended capital stock (baseline)\n- J = Extended capital stock with investment\n- P3 = Capital share (0.35)\n\n**Capital Accumulation** (Production_Function!J):\n```\nK_with = K_with_prev * (1 - δ) + Investment\n```\n\n## Parameters\n\n| Parameter | Value | Location | Notes |\n|-----------|-------|----------|-------|\n| Capital Share (α) | 0.35 | Production_Function!P3 | Typical for emerging markets |\n| Labor Share (1-α) | 0.65 | Production_Function!P5 | Calculated |\n| HP Lambda (λ) | 1600 | Production!Q3 | Standard for annual data |\n| Depreciation (δ) | Auto | Production!B3 | From CFC data |\n| Investment (I) | $6.5B | Investment!B4 | Annual, 2026-2033 |\n\n## Interpretation\n\n### Output Impact Calculation\nThe model calculates:\n\n1. **Baseline Scenario**: Output without investment\n   ```\n   Y* = exp(A_trend) · K^0.35\n   ```\n\n2. **Shock Scenario**: Output with investment\n   ```\n   Y* = exp(A_trend) · K_with^0.35\n   ```\n\n3. **Output Gain**:\n   ```\n   ΔY = Y*_with - Y*_base\n   ```\n\n### Expected Magnitude\n- Capital increase: ~15-20% by 2033\n- Output effect: ~5-7% by 2033 (given α=0.35)\n- Typical return: 0.05-0.15 units of output per dollar invested\n\n## Data Sources\n\n### 1. WEO_Data (IMF World Economic Outlook)\n- **URL**: https://data.imf.org/en/datasets/IMF.RES:WEO\n- **Coverage**: Real GDP growth 2000-2025, projections 2026-2027\n- **Update**: October 2025 release\n- **Usage**: Baseline GDP projections\n\n### 2. CFC data (ECB)\n- **URL**: https://data.ecb.europa.eu/data/geographical-areas/georgia\n- **Coverage**: Consumption of Fixed Capital 1996-2023\n- **Currency**: Georgian Lari (millions)\n- **Update**: May 29, 2024\n- **Usage**: Depreciation rate estimation\n\n### 3. PWT (Penn World Table 11.0)\n- **URL**: https://www.rug.nl/ggdc/productivity/pwt/\n- **Coverage**: Capital stock, TFP 1990-2019\n- **Access**: Via FRED or direct download\n- **Usage**: Capital stock data and productivity\n\n### 4. Investment Shock\n- **Source**: User specification\n- **Parameters**: $6.5B annually, 2026-2033\n- **Assumption**: Full immediate productivity\n\n## Customization\n\n### Change Investment Amount\nEdit **Investment!B4** (currently 6500 = $6.5B)\n\n### Change Investment Period\nEdit **Investment!B5** (start year) and **B6** (duration in years)\n\n### Adjust Capital Share\nEdit **Production_Function!P3** (currently 0.35)\n\n### Extend Projection Period\n1. F8:F29\n\n### Negative Output Gains\n- Check if K_with (column J) properly accumulates investment\n- Verify depreciation rate in B3 is positive\n- Check investment schedule in Investment sheet\n\n## Key Assumptions\n\n1. **Cobb-Douglas Form**: Constant returns to scale with fixed α=0.35\n2. **HP Filtering**: Assumes smooth trend TFP path with λ=1600\n3. **Capital Accumulation**: Linear depreciation, no adjustment costs\n4. **Immediate Productivity**: Investment fully productive in year received\n5. **Labor Supply**: Exogenous or captured in TFP trend\n6. **Extended Growth**: Constant 6.2% growth post-2027\n\n## Limitations\n\n- **HP Filter Sensitivity**: Results depend on λ choice\n- **Limited Data**: PWT ends 2019 (estimates for 2020-2023)\n- **Currency Risk**: USD investment in LR economy\n- **No Structural Change**: Model doesn't account for policy or institutional changes\n- **Forecast Uncertainty**: Extended growth rates (2028-2041) are projections\n\n## Production_Function!N | Annual difference |\n| Capital gain | Production_Function!J-E | Capital stock increase |\n| Cumulative gain | Summary!B17 | Total output gains 2026-2041 |\n\n## Support & This updates the trend TFP (LnZ_Trend)\n\n### 5. δ) + Investment\n```\n\n## Parameters\n\n| Parameter | Value | Location | Notes |\n|-----------|-------|----------|-------|\n| Capital Share (α) | 0.35 | Production_Function!P3 | Typical for emerging markets |\n| Labor Share (1-α) | 0.65 | Production_Function!P5 | Calculated |\n| HP Lambda (λ) | 1600 | Production!Q3 | Standard for annual data |\n| Depreciation (δ) | Auto | Production!B3 | From CFC data |\n| Investment (I) | $6.5B | Investment!B4 | Annual, 2026-2033 |\n\n## Interpretation\n\n### Output Impact Calculation\nThe model calculates:\n\n1. **Baseline Scenario**: Output without investment\n   ```\n   Y* = exp(A_trend) · K^0.35\n   ```\n\n2. **Shock Scenario**: Output with investment\n   ```\n   Y* = exp(A_trend) · K_with^0.35\n   ```\n\n3. **Output Gain**:\n   ```\n   ΔY = Y*_with - Y*_base\n   ```\n\n### Expected Magnitude\n- Capital increase: ~15-20% by 2033\n- Output effect: ~5-7% by 2033 (given α=0.35)\n- Typical return: 0.05-0.15 units of output per dollar invested\n\n## Data Sources\n\n### 1.
